- **cie-institutional-background**: Use when the policy / institutional background section of a 《中国工业经济》 (China Industrial Economics) manuscript needs accurate detail — exact document names, effective dates, pilot batches and city lists, and the assignment rule that justifies the quasi-experiment. - **cie-literature-review**: Use when the literature section of a 《中国工业经济》 (China Industrial Economics) manuscript is a citation pile instead of a critical review that locates the gap. - **cie-mechanism**: Use when a 《中国工业经济》 (China Industrial Economics) manuscript needs mechanism evidence. - **cjc-review-process**: 在需要理解或预判《计算机学报》(Chinese Journal of Computers, CJC) 审稿流程、以便安排投稿节奏与修回策略时调用。覆盖本刊三审制的完整链路(编辑初审→专家外审→复审退修→主编终审)、初审退稿的常见理由(不符宗旨/无新意/重复前人/观点模糊/学术不端总重复率超 30%)、外审的双盲(删除作者信息)与 2~3 名同行评议约 20 天、多轮退修与重新送审、第一轮约 3~4 个月的审理周期与全程约半年的经验时长，以及作者在各环节的可用杠杆。适用于为计算机全学科中文长文规划评审预期的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Computers-Skills/skills/cjc-review-process) · ★1.1k)
- **cjc-submission**: 在向《计算机学报》(Chinese Journal of Computers, CJC) 正式提交稿件前做投稿就绪审计时调用。覆盖官网 cjc.ict.ac.cn 在线投稿系统、注册与提交同步(相差不超过 1 天)规则、官方 Word/LaTeX 长文模板、中英文摘要与关键词、中图分类号(TP)、GB/T 7714 参考文献中英文对照、学术不端检测(总重复率 30% 红线)、审理费与栏目归属，并在提交前做退稿风险分诊。适用于把一份计算机全学科的中文原创长文打磨到符合本刊三审制门槛的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Computers-Skills/skills/cjc-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **cjc-supplementary**: 在为投向《计算机学报》(Chinese Journal of Computers, CJC) 的长文组织附录、补充材料与多媒体附件时调用。覆盖如何在正文长文与补充材料之间按"是否决定录用"划分内容、附录中放证明细节/额外实验/参数表/算法伪码/大图表的取舍原则、补充材料的引用与自洽要求、多媒体附件的格式与容量、以及在双盲外审下补充材料的匿名处理。适用于让一篇计算机全学科中文原创长文主体紧凑有力、支撑材料完备可查的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Computers-Skills/skills/cjc-supplementary) · ★1.1k)
- **cjc-topic-selection**: 在判断一项计算机研究是否适合投《计算机学报》(Chinese Journal of Computers, CJC)、以及应落到哪个栏目/稿件类型时调用。覆盖本刊作为 CCF A 类计算机学科综合性中文旗舰月刊的定位、与专业子刊(软件学报偏软件/系统、自动化学报偏控制、电子学报偏电子通信、计算机研究与发展、计算机科学)的分工、长文与原创性门槛、论文/技术报告/短文/研究简报/综述的类型选择、以及会议论文期刊扩展版的定位。适用于在投稿前把选题的期刊契合度、创新增量与栏目归属想清楚，避免因不符宗旨在初审被退。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Computers-Skills/skills/cjc-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **cjc-workflow**: 在需要为一篇投向《计算机学报》(Chinese Journal of Computers, CJC) 的中文长文规划从选题到见刊的完整流程时调用。把 12 个子技能编排成一条时间线：选题栏目定位→写作与实验搭建→相关工作与创新性确立→投稿自审→三审制评审(初审/外审/复审退修/主编终审)→多轮修回答复→录用定稿与校样→出版，并给出各阶段的检查点、经验周期与并行安排。适用于第一次投本刊、或需要把一次投稿全周期系统化管理、避免在任一环节踩坑的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Computers-Skills/skills/cjc-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **cjc-writing-style**: 在撰写或润色投向《计算机学报》(Chinese Journal of Computers, CJC) 的中文科技长文时调用。覆盖本刊要求的报道性中英文摘要四要素、3~8 个中英文对应关键词、中图分类号(TP 类)的选取、题名规范、正文结构与论证密度、术语与符号统一、量和单位使用法定计量单位、图表题名中英文规范、参考文献 GB/T 7714 著录与中文文献中英文对照，以及"文字精练、无语言文字错误"的编辑级语言要求。适用于把一篇计算机全学科原创研究打磨成符合 CJC 长文体例与三审语言标准的场景。 ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Computers-Skills/skills/cjc-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
- **cjf-jurisprudencia**: Busca jurisprudência unificada no CJF (STF, STJ, TRFs, TNU) via browser-use CLI. Dispara com "jurisprudência", "precedente", "acórdão", "ementa", "CJF", "STJ", "STF", "TRF", ou pesquisa de tese jurídica. ([marcosmarf27/tecjustica](https://github.com/marcosmarf27/tecjustica/tree/HEAD/skills/cjf-jurisprudencia) · ★24)
- **cjms-empirical-validation**: Use when designing the real-data validation of a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — forecasting and financial-engineering strands: data provenance, rolling out-of-sample tests, benchmark batteries, and significance of improvement. Validates methods on data; simulation-based studies belong to cjms-numerical-experiments. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-empirical-validation) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-literature-review**: Use when building the literature review and contribution positioning for a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — running the Chinese and international literatures as two lanes and pinning a nameable methodological increment. Positions the paper; it does not construct the model itself. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-literature-review) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-managerial-insights**: Use when writing the managerial insights and countermeasure suggestions of a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — turning model and experiment results into condition-decision-effect rules instead of policy slogans. Shapes the payoff section; it does not generate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-managerial-insights) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-model-formulation**: Use when translating a real management setting into the formal model of a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — situational elements into variables, constraints, objectives and defensible assumptions, across optimization, game, and data-driven model families. Builds the model; solving it belongs to cjms-solution-algorithm. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-model-formulation) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-numerical-experiments**: Use when designing the numerical experiments and 算例 of a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — grounded parameter calibration, an experiment matrix, sensitivity analysis, and mechanism-level reading of results. For simulation and case computation; real-data method validation belongs to cjms-empirical-validation. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-numerical-experiments) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-rebuttal**: Use when responding to referee reports from 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) — classifying comments, planning supplementary experiments or derivations, and drafting the point-by-point 修改说明. Works after the manuscript has actually been revised; it never fabricates changes. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-rebuttal) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-solution-algorithm**: Use when choosing the solution route and delivering the algorithm section of a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — exact vs heuristic vs learning-based, with pseudocode, property analysis, and baseline comparison. Covers solving the model; the experiments that showcase it belong to cjms-numerical-experiments. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-solution-algorithm) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-submission**: Use when running the pre-submission preflight for a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — the zgglkx.com portal flow, the 12-page limit, fee and timeline caveats, and the full formal checklist. Final gate before submission; it does not rewrite content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-submission) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-tables-figures**: Use when preparing tables, figures, formulas and pseudocode exhibits for a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — three-line tables, equation-editor formulas with unified numbering, algorithm boxes, and convergence/sensitivity plots. Formats exhibits; the analysis behind them belongs to earlier skills. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-tables-figures) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-topic-selection**: Use when choosing or vetting a topic for a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — testing fit with the journal's applied-modeling mission, mapping the paper to a column, and screening out sister-journal mismatches. Judges fit only; it does not build the model or write the review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-topic-selection) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-workflow**: Use when deciding which cjms-* sub-skill to invoke next, or when sequencing a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript from topic selection through rebuttal. The key fork is 优化建模 vs 数据驱动预测 vs 金融工程实证 — three strands the journal publishes side by side. Routes — does not replace — the specialized skills. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-workflow) · ★1.1k)
- **cjms-writing-style**: Use when polishing structure and house style for a 《中国管理科学》 (Chinese Journal of Management Science) manuscript — the prescribed section order, the 100–200-character self-contained abstract, keywords, CLC number, 结语, sequential-coded references, and the English abstract block. Enforces house style; content quality belongs to earlier skills. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Chinese-Journal-of-Management-Science-Skills/skills/cjms-writing-style) · ★1.1k)
- **ck**: OpenAI Codexの永続的なプロジェクト単位のメモリ。セッション開始時にプロジェクトコンテキストを自動読み込み、gitアクティビティでセッションを追跡し、ネイティブメモリに書き込みます。コマンドは決定的なNode.jsスクリプトを実行します — 動作はモデルバージョン間で一貫しています。 ([mturac/everything-openai-codex](https://github.com/mturac/everything-openai-codex/tree/HEAD/docs/ja-JP/skills/ck) · ★91)
- **ck**: OpenAI Codex 的每个项目持久化记忆。在会话启动时自动加载项目上下文，通过 git 活动追踪会话，并写入原生记忆。命令运行确定性的 Node.js 脚本——行为在不同模型版本间保持一致。 ([mturac/everything-openai-codex](https://github.com/mturac/everything-openai-codex/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-CN/skills/ck) · ★91)
- **ck**: Persistent per-project memory for OpenAI Codex. Auto-loads project context on session start, tracks sessions with git activity, and writes to native memory. Commands run deterministic Node.js scripts — behavior is consistent across model versions. ([mturac/everything-openai-codex](https://github.com/mturac/everything-openai-codex/tree/HEAD/skills/ck) · ★91)
- **ck**: Persistent per-project memory for Gemini CLI. Auto-loads project context on session start, tracks sessions with git activity, and writes to native memory. Commands run deterministic Node.js scripts — behavior is consistent across model versions. ([Jamkris/everything-gemini-code](https://github.com/Jamkris/everything-gemini-code/tree/HEAD/skills/ck) · ★87)
- **ck**: Persistent per-project memory for Claude Code. Auto-loads project context on session start, tracks sessions with git activity, and writes to native memory. Commands run deterministic Node.js scripts — behavior is consistent across model versions. ([mit-network/everything-claude-code](https://github.com/mit-network/everything-claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/ck) · ★83 · 57 copies)
- **ck**: Persistent per-project memory for Gemini Code. Auto-loads project context on session start, tracks sessions with git activity, and writes to native memory. Commands run deterministic Node.js scripts: behavior is consistent across model versions. ([Fmarzochi/EGC](https://github.com/Fmarzochi/EGC/tree/HEAD/skills/general/ck) · ★53)
- **ck**: Claude Code 的持久化项目记忆。会话启动时自动加载项目上下文，跟踪带有 git 活动的会话，并写入原生记忆。命令运行确定性的 Node.js 脚本 — 行为在不同模型版本间保持一致。 ([aaione/everything-claude-code-zh](https://github.com/aaione/everything-claude-code-zh/tree/HEAD/skills/ck) · ★29)
- **ck**: Claude Code 的每个项目持久化记忆。在会话启动时自动加载项目上下文，通过 git 活动追踪会话，并写入原生记忆。命令运行确定性的 Node.js 脚本——行为在不同模型版本间保持一致。 ([affaan-m/ECC](https://github.com/affaan-m/ECC/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-CN/skills/ck) · 3.1k installs · 23 copies)
- **ck**: Claude Codeの永続的なプロジェクト単位のメモリ。セッション開始時にプロジェクトコンテキストを自動読み込み、gitアクティビティでセッションを追跡し、ネイティブメモリに書き込みます。コマンドは決定的なNode.jsスクリプトを実行します — 動作はモデルバージョン間で一貫しています。 ([affaan-m/ECC](https://github.com/affaan-m/ECC/tree/HEAD/docs/ja-JP/skills/ck) · 3.1k installs · 19 copies)
- **ck**: Persistent per-project memory for Claude Code. Auto-loads project context on session start, tracks sessions with git activity, and writes to native memory. Commands run deterministic Node.js scripts — behavior is consistent across model versions. Use when a project needs context to survive across Claude Code sessions instead of being re-explained each time. ([affaan-m/ECC](https://github.com/affaan-m/ECC/tree/HEAD/skills/ck) · 3.1k installs · 6 copies)
- **ck-brainstorm**: Explore and debate solutions before writing code. Use when the user poses a design question, asks "how should I approach X", wants to think through options before committing, or says anything like "let's brainstorm", "what's the best way to...", "I'm not sure how to tackle this", or "should I use X or Y". Always runs before /ck:plan for novel or ambiguous features. No code is written — only: explore → clarify → spec → report. ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-brainstorm) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **ck-cook**: Implement a feature phase by phase from a phased JSON master or Markdown plan. Before each phase, confirm whether to create/run unit tests and whether to run ck:quality, unless explicit flags supply those choices. Supports token-budgeted execution, resumable state, and TDD handoff to ck:test. ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-cook) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **ck-fix**: Fix a bug using Scout → Diagnose → Fix → Review → Finalize. Use when the user pastes an error message, stack trace, or test failure, or says "fix this bug", "something's broken", "tests are failing", "I'm getting an error". Also accepts `--from-quality &lt;report&gt;` or `--from-test &lt;report&gt;` to fix exactly the findings/failures a report lists. Modes (pick one): --fast (trivial errors — lint, type, build — skip scout and review), --hard (mandatory review, no auto-approve). ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-fix) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **ck-plan**: Plan a feature or system before implementation. Use when the user says "plan this", "I want to build X", "how do I implement Y", or when /ck:brainstorm produces a spec.md. Always run before /ck:cook. Modes (pick one): --fast (simple, single-file), --hard (research + red-team + validate). Composable flag (combine with any mode): --tdd — propagates into the cook pipeline. Every phase gets Design Constraints and Quality/Testing State; ck:cook confirms optional test and quality checks per phase. ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-plan) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **ck-plan-json**: Create a machine-readable phased JSON plan bundle from a spec or feature description. Produces a compact master manifest plus per-phase step files for resumable ck:cook execution. ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-plan-json) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **ck-quality**: Independent senior-engineering quality auditor — architecture, ownership, domain integrity, abstraction, and maintainability. Use as `--gate` inside the ck:cook pipeline (blocks a phase on BLOCKER/HIGH/current-change MEDIUM findings) or call directly with `--audit`, `--diff`, `--changed` on any repository, directory, or file. Never writes tests and never edits production code — it returns structured findings only; ck:cook or ck:fix apply the fix. ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-quality) · ★19)
- **ck-review**: Review documents, proposals, and plans through the lens of a senior principal engineer. Simulates feedback from a technically deep IC who values clarity, pragmatism, and shipping velocity. Use when you want direct, blunt technical feedback on a proposal, architecture doc, design doc, or spec — especially around structure, tradeoffs, and scope. ([ckorhonen/claude-skills](https://github.com/ckorhonen/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ck-review) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **ck-test**: Standalone testing skill — owns writing and running tests, verifying behavior, and two-pass TDD orchestration. Default mode analyzes changed scope and runs the relevant tests; `--unit`/`--integration`/`--e2e`/`--all` scope by test type; `--verify` reruns exactly the prior failures; `--all-phases &lt;plan&gt;` sweeps every completed phase for regression. `--tdd --prepare`/`--tdd --verify` bracket `ck:cook` for red-green TDD. Never edits production code, and blocks until a phase's `ck:quality` gate is APPROVED (except `--tdd --prepare`, which runs before any implementation exists). ([DangQuangSE/team-development-skills](https://github.com/DangQuangSE/team-development-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/ck-test) · ★19 · 2 copies)
- **ck:ai-multimodal**: Analyze images/audio/video with Gemini API (better vision than Claude). Generate images (Imagen 4, Nano Banana 2, MiniMax), videos (Veo 3, Hailuo), speech (MiniMax TTS), music (MiniMax). Use for vision analysis, transcription, OCR, design extraction, multimodal AI. ([binjuhor/shadcn-lar](https://github.com/binjuhor/shadcn-lar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/backend-development) · ★100 · 6 copies)

([binjuhor/shadcn-lar](https://github.com/binjuhor/shadcn-lar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/better-auth) · ★100 · 6 copies)

Always runs before /ck:plan for novel or ambiguous features. No code is written — only: explore → clarify → spec → report. - **ck:chrome-devtools**: Automate browsers with Puppeteer CLI scripts and persistent sessions. - **ck:code-review**: Review code quality with adversarial rigor. Supports input modes: pending changes, PR number, commit hash, codebase scan. Always-on red-team analysis finds security holes, false assumptions, and failure modes. ([binjuhor/shadcn-lar](https://github.com/binjuhor/shadcn-lar/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/code-review) · ★100 · 7 copies)
